# PROGRAMMING MASTERY QUESTION BANK — ISKONNECT

> **Source:** [`ENGINEERING_KNOWLEDGE_PORTFOLIO.md`](ENGINEERING_KNOWLEDGE_PORTFOLIO.md) only.
> **Purpose:** Active recall — no answers. Rebuild the backend from memory.
> **Rule:** After every question, fill in the confidence tracker.

## How to Use This Document

1. Pick one **Topic**.
2. Start at **LEVEL 1** — rapid fire without notes.
3. Move up levels only when LEVEL 1–3 feel automatic.
4. **LEVEL 13** means empty editor — no peeking.
5. Spaced repetition: review `Next Review Date` weekly for scores ≤3.

## Spaced Repetition Schedule (Suggested)

| Confidence | Review interval |
|---|---|
| 1–2 | Next day |
| 3 | 3 days |
| 4 | 7 days |
| 5 | 21 days |
